12. using System;
13. using Windows.Devices.Geolocation;
14. using Windows.Foundation;
15. using Windows.UI.Core;
16. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;
17. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
18. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
19. 
20. namespace SDKTemplate
21. {
22.     public sealed partial class Scenario1_TrackPosition : Page
23.     {
24.         // Proides access to location data
25.         private Geolocator _geolocator = null;
26. 
27.         // A pointer to the main page
28.         private MainPage _rootPage = MainPage.Current;
29. 
30.         public Scenario1_TrackPosition()
31.         {
32.             this.InitializeComponent();
33.         }
34. 
35.         protected override void OnNavigatedFrom(NavigationEventArgs e)
36.         {
37.             if (_geolocator != null)
38.             {
39.                 _geolocator.PositionChanged -= OnPositionChanged;
40.                 _geolocator.StatusChanged -= OnStatusChanged;
41.             }
42.         }
43. 
44.         /// <summary>
45.         /// This is the click handler for the 'StartTracking' button.
46.         /// </summary>
47.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
48.         /// <param name="e"></param>
49.         private async void StartTracking(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
50.         {
51.             // Request permission to access location
52.             var accessStatus = await Geolocator.RequestAccessAsync();
53. 
54.             switch (accessStatus)
55.             {
56.                 case GeolocationAccessStatus.Allowed:
57.                     // You should set MovementThreshold for distance-based tracking
58.                     // or ReportInterval for periodic-based tracking before adding event
59.                     // handlers. If none is set, a ReportInterval of 1 second is used
60.                     // as a default and a position will be returned every 1 second.
61.                     //
62.                     // Value of 2000 milliseconds (2 seconds)
63.                     // isn't a requirement, it is just an example.
64.                     _geolocator = new Geolocator { ReportInterval = 2000 };
65. 
66.                     // Subscribe to PositionChanged event to get updated tracking positions
67.                     _geolocator.PositionChanged += OnPositionChanged;
68. 
69.                     // Subscribe to StatusChanged event to get updates of location status changes
70.                     _geolocator.StatusChanged += OnStatusChanged;
71. 
72.                     _rootPage.NotifyUser("Waiting for update...", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
73.                     LocationDisabledMessage.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
74.                     StartTrackingButton.IsEnabled = false;
75.                     StopTrackingButton.IsEnabled = true;
76.                     break;
77. 
78.                 case GeolocationAccessStatus.Denied:
79.                     _rootPage.NotifyUser("Access to location is denied.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
80.                     LocationDisabledMessage.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
81.                     break;
82. 
83.                 case GeolocationAccessStatus.Unspecified:
84.                     _rootPage.NotifyUser("Unspecificed error!", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
85.                     LocationDisabledMessage.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
86.                     break;
87.             }
88.         }
89. 
90.         /// <summary>
91.         /// This is the click handler for the 'StopTracking' button.
92.         /// </summary>
93.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
94.         /// <param name="e"></param>
95.         private void StopTracking(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
96.         {
97.             _geolocator.PositionChanged -= OnPositionChanged;
98.             _geolocator.StatusChanged -= OnStatusChanged;
99.             _geolocator = null;
100. 
101.             StartTrackingButton.IsEnabled = true;
102.             StopTrackingButton.IsEnabled = false;
103. 
104.             // Clear status
105.             _rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
106.         }
107. 
108.         /// <summary>
109.         /// Event handler for PositionChanged events. It is raised when
110.         /// a location is available for the tracking session specified.
111.         /// </summary>
112.         /// <param name="sender">Geolocator instance</param>
113.         /// <param name="e">Position data</param>
114.         async private void OnPositionChanged(Geolocator sender, PositionChangedEventArgs e)
115.         {
116.             // TODO[migrate-001]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
117.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
118.             {
119.                 _rootPage.NotifyUser("Location updated.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
120.                 UpdateLocationData(e.Position);
121.             });
122.         }
123. 
124.         /// <summary>
125.         /// Event handler for StatusChanged events. It is raised when the
126.         /// location status in the system changes.
127.         /// </summary>
128.         /// <param name="sender">Geolocator instance</param>
129.         /// <param name="e">Statu data</param>
130.         async private void OnStatusChanged(Geolocator sender, StatusChangedEventArgs e)
131.         {
132.             // TODO[migrate-002]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
133.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
134.             {
135.                 // Show the location setting message only if status is disabled.
136.                 LocationDisabledMessage.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
137. 
138.                 switch (e.Status)
139.                 {
140.                     case PositionStatus.Ready:
141.                         // Location platform is providing valid data.
142.                         ScenarioOutput_Status.Text = "Ready";
143.                         _rootPage.NotifyUser("Location platform is ready.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
144.                         break;
145. 
146.                     case PositionStatus.Initializing:
147.                         // Location platform is attempting to acquire a fix.
148.                         ScenarioOutput_Status.Text = "Initializing";
149.                         _rootPage.NotifyUser("Location platform is attempting to obtain a position.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
150.                         break;
151. 
152.                     case PositionStatus.NoData:
153.                         // Location platform could not obtain location data.
154.                         ScenarioOutput_Status.Text = "No data";
155.                         _rootPage.NotifyUser("Not able to determine the location.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
156.                         break;
157. 
158.                     case PositionStatus.Disabled:
159.                         // The permission to access location data is denied by the user or other policies.
160.                         ScenarioOutput_Status.Text = "Disabled";
161.                         _rootPage.NotifyUser("Access to location is denied.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
162. 
163.                         // Show message to the user to go to location settings
164.                         LocationDisabledMessage.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
165. 
166.                         // Clear cached location data if any
167.                         UpdateLocationData(null);
168.                         break;
169. 
170.                     case PositionStatus.NotInitialized:
171.                         // The location platform is not initialized. This indicates that the application
172.                         // has not made a request for location data.
173.                         ScenarioOutput_Status.Text = "Not initialized";
174.                         _rootPage.NotifyUser("No request for location is made yet.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
175.                         break;
176. 
177.                     case PositionStatus.NotAvailable:
178.                         // The location platform is not available on this version of the OS.
179.                         ScenarioOutput_Status.Text = "Not available";
180.                         _rootPage.NotifyUser("Location is not available on this version of the OS.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
181.                         break;
182. 
183.                     default:
184.                         ScenarioOutput_Status.Text = "Unknown";
185.                         _rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
186.                         break;
187.                 }
188.             });
189.         }
190. 
191.         /// <summary>
192.         /// Updates the user interface with the Geoposition data provided
193.         /// </summary>
194.         /// <param name="position">Geoposition to display its details</param>
195.         private void UpdateLocationData(Geoposition position)
196.         {
197.             if (position == null)
198.             {
199.                 ScenarioOutput_Latitude.Text = "No data";
200.                 ScenarioOutput_Longitude.Text = "No data";
201.                 ScenarioOutput_Accuracy.Text = "No data";
202.                 ScenarioOutput_Source.Text = "No data";
203.                 ScenarioOutput_IsRemoteSource.Text = "No data";
204.             }
205.             else
206.             {
207.                 ScenarioOutput_Latitude.Text = position.Coordinate.Point.Position.Latitude.ToString();
208.                 ScenarioOutput_Longitude.Text = position.Coordinate.Point.Position.Longitude.ToString();
209.                 ScenarioOutput_Accuracy.Text = position.Coordinate.Accuracy.ToString();
210.                 ScenarioOutput_Source.Text = position.Coordinate.PositionSource.ToString();
211.                 ScenarioOutput_IsRemoteSource.Text = position.Coordinate.IsRemoteSource.ToString();

24.     public sealed partial class Scenario3_BackgroundTask : Page
25.     {
26.         // For background task registration
27.         private const string BackgroundTaskName = "SampleLocationBackgroundTask";
28.         private const string BackgroundTaskEntryPoint = "BackgroundTask.LocationBackgroundTask";
29. 
30.         private IBackgroundTaskRegistration _geolocTask = null;
31. 
32.         // A pointer to the main page
33.         private MainPage _rootPage = MainPage.Current;
34. 
35.         public Scenario3_BackgroundTask()
36.         {
37.             this.InitializeComponent();
38.         }
39. 
40.         /// <summary>
41.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
42.         /// </summary>
43.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached. The Parameter
44.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
45.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
46.         {
47.             // See if our background task is already registered.
48.             _geolocTask = MainPage.LookupBackgroundTask(BackgroundTaskName);
49. 
50.             if (_geolocTask != null)
51.             {
52.                 // Associate an event handler with the existing background task
53.                 _geolocTask.Completed += OnCompleted;
54.             }
55.             UpdateButtonStates();
56.         }
57. 
58.         protected override void OnNavigatedFrom(NavigationEventArgs e)
59.         {
60.             if (_geolocTask != null)
61.             {
62.                 // Remove the event handler
63.                 _geolocTask.Completed -= OnCompleted;
64.             }
65.         }
66. 
67.         /// <summary>
68.         /// This is the click handler for the 'Register' button.
69.         /// </summary>
70.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
71.         /// <param name="e"></param>
72.         private void RegisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
73.         {
74.             // Register the background task without checking whether the user has enabled background execution.
75.             // If it's disabled, and then the user later enables background execution, the background task will be ready to run.
76.             // Create a new background task builder
77.             // TODO[migrate-003]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
78.             BackgroundTaskBuilder geolocTaskBuilder = new BackgroundTaskBuilder();
79. 
80.             geolocTaskBuilder.Name = BackgroundTaskName;
81.             geolocTaskBuilder.TaskEntryPoint = BackgroundTaskEntryPoint;
82. 
83.             // Create a new timer triggering at a 15 minute interval
84.             var trigger = new TimeTrigger(15, false);
85. 
86.             // Associate the timer trigger with the background task builder
87.             geolocTaskBuilder.SetTrigger(trigger);
88. 
89.             // Register the background task
90.             _geolocTask = geolocTaskBuilder.Register();
91. 
92.             // Associate an event handler with the new background task
93.             _geolocTask.Completed += OnCompleted;
94. 
95.             UpdateButtonStates();
96.             MainPage.CheckBackgroundAndRequestLocationAccess();
97.         }
98. 
99.         /// <summary>
100.         /// This is the click handler for the 'Unregister' button.
101.         /// </summary>
102.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
103.         /// <param name="e"></param>
104.         private void UnregisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
105.         {
106.             // Unregister the background task
107.             if (null != _geolocTask)
108.             {
109.                 _geolocTask.Unregister(true);
110.                 _geolocTask = null;
111.             }
112. 
113.             ScenarioOutput_Latitude.Text = "No data";
114.             ScenarioOutput_Longitude.Text = "No data";
115.             ScenarioOutput_Accuracy.Text = "No data";
116.             UpdateButtonStates();
117.             _rootPage.NotifyUser("Background task unregistered", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
118.         }
119. 
120.         /// <summary>
121.         /// Event handler called when the background task is completed
122.         /// </summary>
123.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
124.         /// <param name="e"></param>
125.         private async void OnCompleted(IBackgroundTaskRegistration sender, BackgroundTaskCompletedEventArgs e)
126.         {
127.             // Update the UI with progress reported by the background task
128.             // TODO[migrate-004]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
129.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
130.             {
131.                 try
132.                 {
133.                     // Rethrow any exception that occurred in the background task.
134.                     e.CheckResult();
135. 
136.                     // Update the UI with the completion status of the background task
137.                     // The Run method of the background task sets this status.
138.                     MainPage.ReportSavedStatus();
139. 
140.                     // Extract and display location data set by the background task if not null
141.                     ScenarioOutput_Latitude.Text = MainPage.LookupSavedString("Latitude", "No data");
142.                     ScenarioOutput_Longitude.Text = MainPage.LookupSavedString("Longitude", "No data");
143.                     ScenarioOutput_Accuracy.Text = MainPage.LookupSavedString("Accuracy", "No data");
144.                 }
145.                 catch (Exception ex)
146.                 {
147.                     // The background task had an error
148.                     _rootPage.NotifyUser(ex.ToString(), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
149.                 }
150.             });
151.         }
152. 
153.         /// <summary>
154.         /// Update the enable state of the register/unregister buttons.
155.         /// </summary>
156.         private void UpdateButtonStates()
157.         {
158.             bool registered = (_geolocTask != null);
159.             RegisterBackgroundTaskButton.IsEnabled = !registered;
160.             UnregisterBackgroundTaskButton.IsEnabled = registered;
161.         }
162.     }

48.         protected override async void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
49.         {
50.             // TODO[migrate-005]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
51.             Window.Current.VisibilityChanged += OnVisibilityChanged;
52. 
53.             DateTime oneHourLater = DateTime.Now.AddHours(1);
54.             //rjc/$$
55.             StartDate.Date = oneHourLater.Date;
56.             StartTime.Time = oneHourLater.TimeOfDay;
57. 
58.             // Get permission to use location
59.             GeolocationAccessStatus accessStatus = await Geolocator.RequestAccessAsync();
60.             switch (accessStatus)
61.             {
62.                 case GeolocationAccessStatus.Allowed:
63.                     // Wrap in try/catch in case user revokes access suddenly.
64.                     try
65.                     {
66.                         geofenceMonitor = GeofenceMonitor.Current;
67.                     }
68.                     catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
69.                     {
70.                         _rootPage.NotifyUser("Access denied.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
71.                     }
72.                     if (geofenceMonitor != null)
73.                     {
74.                         foreach (Geofence geofence in geofenceMonitor.Geofences)
75.                         {
76.                             AddGeofenceToRegisteredGeofenceListBox(geofence);
77.                         }
78. 
79.                         // register for state change events
80.                         geofenceMonitor.GeofenceStateChanged += OnGeofenceStateChanged;
81.                         geofenceMonitor.StatusChanged += OnGeofenceStatusChanged;
82.                     }
83.                     break;
84. 
85.                 case GeolocationAccessStatus.Denied:
86.                     _rootPage.NotifyUser("Access denied.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
87.                     break;
88. 
89.                 case GeolocationAccessStatus.Unspecified:
90.                     _rootPage.NotifyUser("Unspecified error.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
91.                     break;
92.             }

148.         public async void OnGeofenceStateChanged(GeofenceMonitor sender, object e)
149.         {
150.             var reports = sender.ReadReports();
151. 
152.             // TODO[migrate-008]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
153.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
154.             {
155.                 foreach (GeofenceStateChangeReport report in reports)
156.                 {
157.                     GeofenceState state = report.NewState;
158.                     Geofence geofence = report.Geofence;
159.                     string eventDescription = geofence.Id + " " + DateTime.Now.ToString("T") + " (" + state.ToString();
160. 
161.                     if (state == GeofenceState.Removed)
162.                     {
163.                         eventDescription += "/" + report.RemovalReason.ToString() + ")";
164.                         AddEventDescription(eventDescription);
165. 
166.                         // remove the geofence from the monitor
167.                         geofenceMonitor.Geofences.Remove(geofence);
168. 
169.                         // Remove the geofence from the list box.
170.                         foreach (ListBoxItem item in RegisteredGeofenceListBox.Items)
171.                         {
172.                             if (item.Tag == geofence)
173.                             {
174.                                 RegisteredGeofenceListBox.Items.Remove(item);
175.                                 break;
176.                             }
177.                         }
178.                     }
179.                     else if (state == GeofenceState.Entered || state == GeofenceState.Exited)
180.                     {
181.                         // NOTE: You might want to write your app to take particular
182.                         // action based on whether the app has internet connectivity.
183. 
184.                         eventDescription += ")";
185.                         AddEventDescription(eventDescription);
186.                     }
187.                 }
188.             });
189.         }

211.         private Geofence GenerateGeofence()
212.         {
213.             _rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
214. 
215.             string fenceKey = Id.Text;
216.             if (fenceKey.Length == 0)
217.             {
218.                 _rootPage.NotifyUser("Name must be between 1 and 64 characters.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
219.                 return null;
220.             }
221. 
222.             BasicGeoposition position;
223.             double radius;
224.             if (!ParseDoubleFromTextBox(Longitude, "Longitude", -180, 180, out position.Longitude) ||
225.                 !ParseDoubleFromTextBox(Latitude, "Latitude", -90, 90, out position.Latitude) ||
226.                 !ParseDoubleFromTextBox(Radius, "Radius", .1, 10018754.3, out radius))
227.             {
228.                 return null;
229.             }
230. 
231.             position.Altitude = 0.0;
232. 
233.             // the geofence is a circular region
234.             Geocircle geocircle = new Geocircle(position, radius);
235. 
236.             bool singleUse = SingleUse.IsChecked.Value;
237. 
238.             // want to listen for enter geofence, exit geofence and remove geofence events
239.             // you can select a subset of these event states
240.             MonitoredGeofenceStates mask = MonitoredGeofenceStates.Entered | MonitoredGeofenceStates.Exited | MonitoredGeofenceStates.Removed;
241. 
242.             // Dwell time is how long you need to be in geofence to have been considered to have Entered it.
243.             TimeSpan dwellTime;
244.             if (!ParseTimeSpan(DwellTime.Text, defaultDwellTime, out dwellTime))
245.             {
246.                 _rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid Dwell Time.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
247.                 return null;
248.             }
249. 
250.             // The Duration is the length of time the geofence is active.
251.             // Zero means "infinite duration".
252.             TimeSpan duration;
253.             if (!ParseTimeSpan(Duration.Text, TimeSpan.Zero, out duration))
254.             {
255.                 _rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid Duration.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
256.                 return null;
257.             }
258. 
259.             // setting up the start time of the geofence
260.             DateTimeOffset startTime;
261.             if (StartImmediately.IsChecked.Value)
262.             {
263.                 // The special "start immediately" value can be used if the duration is zero.
264.                 // Otherwise, we get the current time as the start time.
265.                 startTime = (duration == TimeSpan.Zero) ? startImmediately : DateTimeOffset.Now;
266.             }
267.             else
268.             {
269.                 startTime = StartDate.Date + StartTime.Time;
270.             }
271. 
272.             // Let the platform detect other invalid parameter combinations.
273.             try
274.             {
275.                 return new Geofence(fenceKey, geocircle, mask, singleUse, dwellTime, startTime, duration);
276.             }
277.             catch (Exception ex)
278.             {
279.                 _rootPage.NotifyUser(ex.Message, NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
280.                 return null;
281.             }
282.         }
283. 
284.         void OnRegisteredGeofenceListBoxSelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
285.         {
286.             if (0 == e.AddedItems.Count)
287.             {
288.                 // disable the remove button
289.                 RemoveGeofenceItem.IsEnabled = false;
290.             }
291.             else
292.             {
293.                 // enable the remove button
294.                 RemoveGeofenceItem.IsEnabled = true;
295. 
296.                 // update controls with the values from the newly-selected geofence
297.                 Geofence geofence = (Geofence)(RegisteredGeofenceListBox.SelectedItem as ListBoxItem).Tag;
298.                 RefreshControlsFromGeofence(geofence);
299.             }
300.         }

302.         private void RefreshControlsFromGeofence(Geofence geofence)
303.         {
304.             Id.Text = geofence.Id;
305. 
306.             Geocircle circle = (Geocircle)geofence.Geoshape;
307. 
308.             Latitude.Text = circle.Center.Latitude.ToString();
309.             Longitude.Text = circle.Center.Longitude.ToString();
310.             Radius.Text = circle.Radius.ToString();
311.             SingleUse.IsChecked = geofence.SingleUse;
312. 
313.             if (geofence.DwellTime != TimeSpan.Zero)
314.             {
315.                 DwellTime.Text = geofence.DwellTime.ToString(timeSpanFormat);
316.             }
317.             else
318.             {
319.                 DwellTime.Text = "";
320.             }
321. 
322.             if (geofence.Duration != TimeSpan.Zero)
323.             {
324.                 Duration.Text = geofence.Duration.ToString(timeSpanFormat);
325.             }
326.             else
327.             {
328.                 Duration.Text = "";
329.             }
330. 
331.             if (geofence.StartTime == startImmediately)
332.             {
333.                 StartImmediately.IsChecked = true;
334.             }
335.             else
336.             {
337.                 StartAtSpecificTime.IsChecked = true;
338.                 StartDate.Date = geofence.StartTime.Date;
339.                 StartTime.Time = geofence.StartTime.TimeOfDay;
340.             }
341.         }

343.         private bool ParseDoubleFromTextBox(TextBox e, string name, double minValue, double maxValue, out double value)
344.         {
345.             if (!Double.TryParse(e.Text, out value) || (value < minValue) || (value > maxValue))
346.             {
347.                 _rootPage.NotifyUser($"{name} must be between {minValue} and {maxValue}", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
348.                 return false;
349.             }
350.             return true;
351.         }

353.         // add geofence to listbox
354.         private void AddGeofenceToRegisteredGeofenceListBox(Geofence geofence)
355.         {
356.             Geocircle circle = (Geocircle)geofence.Geoshape;
357. 
358.             string text = $"{geofence.Id} ({circle.Center.Latitude:0.###}, {circle.Center.Longitude:0.###}, {circle.Radius:0.#})";
359.             RegisteredGeofenceListBox.Items.Insert(0, new ListBoxItem { Content = text, Tag = geofence });
360.         }

362.         /// <summary>
363.         /// This is the click handler for the 'Set Lat/Long to current position' button.
364.         /// </summary>
365.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
366.         /// <param name="e"></param>
367.         async private void OnSetPositionToHere(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
368.         {
369.             SetPositionProgressBar.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
370.             SetPositionToHereButton.IsEnabled = false;
371.             Latitude.IsEnabled = false;
372.             Longitude.IsEnabled = false;
373. 
374.             try
375.             {
376.                 // Get cancellation token
377.                 _cts = new CancellationTokenSource();
378.                 CancellationToken token = _cts.Token;
379. 
380.                 // Carry out the operation
381.                 // geolocator and location permissions are initialized and checked on page creation.
382.                 var geolocator = new Geolocator();
383. 
384.                 // Request a high accuracy position for better accuracy locating the geofence
385.                 geolocator.DesiredAccuracy = PositionAccuracy.High;
386. 
387.                 Geoposition pos = await geolocator.GetGeopositionAsync().AsTask(token);
388. 
389.                 Latitude.Text = pos.Coordinate.Point.Position.Latitude.ToString();
390.                 Longitude.Text = pos.Coordinate.Point.Position.Longitude.ToString();
391. 
392.                 // clear status
393.                 _rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
394.             }
395.             catch (TaskCanceledException)
396.             {
397.                 _rootPage.NotifyUser("Task canceled", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
398.             }
399.             catch (Exception ex)
400.             {
401.                 _rootPage.NotifyUser(ex.ToString(), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
402.             }
403.             finally
404.             {
405.                 _cts = null;
406.             }
407. 
408.             SetPositionProgressBar.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
409.             SetPositionToHereButton.IsEnabled = true;
410.             Latitude.IsEnabled = true;
411.             Longitude.IsEnabled = true;
412.         }
413. 
414.         /// <summary>
415.         /// This is the click handler for the 'Create Geofence' button.
416.         /// </summary>
417.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
418.         /// <param name="e"></param>
419.         private void OnCreateGeofence(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
420.         {
421.             Geofence geofence = GenerateGeofence();
422.             if (geofence != null)
423.             {
424.                 try
425.                 {
426.                     // add geofence to GeofenceMonitor
427.                     geofenceMonitor.Geofences.Add(geofence);
428.                 }
429.                 catch (Exception ex)
430.                 {
431.                     // GeofenceMonitor failed to add the geofence. Possible reasons include
432.                     // duplicate or invalid name, invalid position, having a limited-duration
433.                     // geofence that doesn't leave enough time for the DwellTime to trigger the geofence.
434.                     _rootPage.NotifyUser(ex.ToString(), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
435.                     geofence = null;
436.                 }
437.             }
438. 
439.             if (geofence != null)
440.             {
441.                 // Update UI after geofence successfully added to GeofenceMonitor.
442.                 AddGeofenceToRegisteredGeofenceListBox(geofence);
443.             }
444.         }

446.         private void AddEventDescription(string eventDescription)
447.         {
448.             GeofenceEventsListBox.Items.Insert(0, new ListBoxItem { Content = eventDescription });
449.         }
450. 
451.         private bool ParseTimeSpan(string field, TimeSpan defaultValue, out TimeSpan value)
452.         {
453.             value = TimeSpan.Zero;
454.             if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(field))
455.             {
456.                 uint[] fields = new uint[4];
457.                 string[] rawFields = field.Split(new[] { ':' });
458.                 int count;
459. 
460.                 for (count = 0; count < rawFields.Length; count++)
461.                 {
462.                     if (!uint.TryParse(rawFields[count], out fields[count]))
463.                     {
464.                         // Not parseable.
465.                         return false;
466.                     }
467.                 }
468. 
469.                 Array.Reverse(fields, 0, count);
470. 
471.                 value = TimeSpan.FromDays(fields[3]) +
472.                     TimeSpan.FromHours(fields[2]) +
473.                     TimeSpan.FromMinutes(fields[1]) +
474.                     TimeSpan.FromSeconds(fields[0]);
475.             }
476. 
477.             if (value == TimeSpan.Zero)
478.             {
479.                 value = defaultValue;
480.             }
481. 
482.             return true;
483.         }
484.     }

23.     public sealed partial class Scenario5_GeofenceBackgroundTask : Page
24.     {
25.         private const string BackgroundTaskName = "SampleGeofenceBackgroundTask";
26.         private const string BackgroundTaskEntryPoint = "BackgroundTask.GeofenceBackgroundTask";
27. 
28.         private IBackgroundTaskRegistration _geofenceTask = null;
29. 
30.         // A pointer to the main page.
31.         private MainPage _rootPage = MainPage.Current;
32. 
33.         public Scenario5_GeofenceBackgroundTask()
34.         {
35.             this.InitializeComponent();
36.         }
37. 
38.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
39.         {
40.             // See if our background task is already registered.
41.             _geofenceTask = MainPage.LookupBackgroundTask(BackgroundTaskName);
42. 
43.             if (_geofenceTask != null)
44.             {
45.                 // Associate an event handler with the existing background task
46.                 _geofenceTask.Completed += OnCompleted;
47. 
48.                 FillEventListBoxWithExistingEvents();
49.             }
50.             UpdateButtonStates();
51.         }
52. 
53.         /// <summary>
54.         /// This is the click handler for the 'Register' button.
55.         /// </summary>
56.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
57.         /// <param name="e"></param>
58.         private void RegisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
59.         {
60.             // Register the background task without checking whether the user has enabled background execution.
61.             // If it's disabled, and then the user later enables background execution, the background task will be ready to run.
62.             // Create a new background task builder
63.             // TODO[migrate-009]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
64.             BackgroundTaskBuilder geofenceTaskBuilder = new BackgroundTaskBuilder();
65. 
66.             geofenceTaskBuilder.Name = BackgroundTaskName;
67.             geofenceTaskBuilder.TaskEntryPoint = BackgroundTaskEntryPoint;
68. 
69.             // Create a new location trigger
70.             var trigger = new LocationTrigger(LocationTriggerType.Geofence);
71. 
72.             // Associate the location trigger with the background task builder
73.             geofenceTaskBuilder.SetTrigger(trigger);
74. 
75.             // If it is important that there is user presence and/or
76.             // internet connection when OnCompleted is called
77.             // the following could be called before calling Register()
78.             // SystemCondition condition = new SystemCondition(SystemConditionType.UserPresent | SystemConditionType.InternetAvailable);
79.             // geofenceTaskBuilder.AddCondition(condition);
80. 
81.             // Register the background task
82.             _geofenceTask = geofenceTaskBuilder.Register();
83. 
84.             // Associate an event handler with the new background task
85.             _geofenceTask.Completed += OnCompleted;
86. 
87.             UpdateButtonStates();
88.             MainPage.CheckBackgroundAndRequestLocationAccess();
89.         }
90. 
91.         /// <summary>
92.         /// This is the click handler for the 'Unregister' button.
93.         /// </summary>
94.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
95.         /// <param name="e"></param>
96.         private void UnregisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
97.         {
98.             // Unregister the background task
99.             if (null != _geofenceTask)
100.             {
101.                 _geofenceTask.Unregister(true);
102.                 _geofenceTask.Completed -= OnCompleted;
103.                 _geofenceTask = null;
104.             }
105. 
106.             _rootPage.NotifyUser("Geofence background task unregistered", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
107.             UpdateButtonStates();
108.         }
109. 
110.         /// <summary>
111.         /// This is the callback when background event has been handled
112.         /// </summary>
113.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
114.         /// <param name="e"></param>
115.         async private void OnCompleted(IBackgroundTaskRegistration sender, BackgroundTaskCompletedEventArgs e)
116.         {
117.             // Update the UI with progress reported by the background task
118.             // TODO[migrate-010]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
119.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
120.             {
121.                 try
122.                 {
123.                     // Rethrow any exception that occurred in the background task.
124.                     e.CheckResult();
125. 
126.                     // Update the UI with the completion status of the background task
127.                     // The Run method of the background task sets this status.
128.                     MainPage.ReportSavedStatus();
129. 
130.                     FillEventListBoxWithExistingEvents();
131.                 }
132.                 catch (Exception ex)
133.                 {
134.                     // The background task had an error
135.                     _rootPage.NotifyUser(ex.ToString(), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
136.                 }
137.             });
138.         }
139.

141.         /// Update the enable state of the register/unregister buttons.
142.         /// </summary>
143.         private void UpdateButtonStates()
144.         {
145.             bool registered = (_geofenceTask != null);
146.             RegisterBackgroundTaskButton.IsEnabled = !registered;
147.             UnregisterBackgroundTaskButton.IsEnabled = registered;
148.         }

23.     public sealed partial class Scenario7_ForegroundVisits : Page
24.     {
25.         // Provides access to visits
26.         private GeovisitMonitor _visitMonitor = null;
27. 
28.         // A pointer to the main page
29.         private MainPage _rootPage = MainPage.Current;
30. 
31.         public Scenario7_ForegroundVisits()
32.         {
33.             this.InitializeComponent();
34.         }
35. 
36.         protected override void OnNavigatedFrom(NavigationEventArgs e)
37.         {
38.             if (_visitMonitor != null)
39.             {
40.                 _visitMonitor.Stop();
41.                 _visitMonitor.VisitStateChanged -= OnVisitStateChanged;
42.             }
43.         }
44. 
45.         /// <summary>
46.         /// This is the click handler for the 'StartMonitoring' button.
47.         /// </summary>
48.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
49.         /// <param name="e"></param>
50.         private async void StartMonitoring(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
51.         {
52.             // Request permission to access location
53.             var accessStatus = await Geolocator.RequestAccessAsync();
54. 
55.             switch (accessStatus)
56.             {
57.                 case GeolocationAccessStatus.Allowed:
58. 
59.                     _visitMonitor = new GeovisitMonitor();
60. 
61.                     // Add visit state changed event handler.
62.                     _visitMonitor.VisitStateChanged += OnVisitStateChanged;
63. 
64.                     // Start monitoring with the desired scope.
65.                     // For higher granularity such as building/venue level changes, choose venue.
66.                     // For lower granularity more or less in the range of zipcode level changes, choose city.
67.                     // Choosing Venue here as an example.
68.                     _visitMonitor.Start(VisitMonitoringScope.Venue);
69. 
70.                     LocationDisabledMessage.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
71.                     _rootPage.NotifyUser("Waiting for update...", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
72. 
73.                     StartMonitoringButton.IsEnabled = false;
74.                     StopMonitoringButton.IsEnabled = true;
75.                     break;
76. 
77.                 case GeolocationAccessStatus.Denied:
78.                     _rootPage.NotifyUser("Access to location is denied.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
79.                     LocationDisabledMessage.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
80.                     break;
81. 
82.                 default:
83.                 case GeolocationAccessStatus.Unspecified:
84.                     _rootPage.NotifyUser("Unspecificed error!", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
85.                     LocationDisabledMessage.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
86.                     break;
87.             }
88.         }
89. 
90.         /// <summary>
91.         /// This is the click handler for the 'StopMonitoring' button.
92.         /// </summary>
93.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
94.         /// <param name="e"></param>
95.         private void StopMonitoring(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
96.         {
97.             // Stop visit monitoring.
98.             _visitMonitor.Stop();
99.             _visitMonitor.VisitStateChanged -= OnVisitStateChanged;
100.             _visitMonitor = null;
101. 
102.             StartMonitoringButton.IsEnabled = true;
103.             StopMonitoringButton.IsEnabled = false;
104. 
105.             // Clear status
106.             _rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
107.         }
108. 
109.         /// <summary>
110.         /// Event handler for VisitStateChanged events. It is raised when
111.         /// a new visit state change is available based on the monitoring scope specified.
112.         /// </summary>
113.         /// <param name="sender">Geovisitmonitor instance</param>
114.         /// <param name="args">VisitStateChanged args data</param>
115.         async private void OnVisitStateChanged(GeovisitMonitor sender, GeovisitStateChangedEventArgs args)
116.         {
117.             // TODO[migrate-011]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
118.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
119.             {
120.                 _rootPage.NotifyUser("Visit state changed.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
121.                 UpdateVisitData(args.Visit);
122.             });
123.         }
124. 
125.         /// <summary>
126.         /// Updates the user interface with the Geovisit data provided
127.         /// </summary>
128.         /// <param name="visit">Geovisit to display its details</param>
129.         private void UpdateVisitData(Geovisit visit)
130.         {
131.             // A valid visit is available, extract the state change and Timestamp.
132.             ScenarioOutput_VisitStateChange.Text = visit.StateChange.ToString();
133.             ScenarioOutput_Timestamp.Text = visit.Timestamp.ToString();
134. 
135.             // If a valid position is available, extract the position information that caused the state change to happen.
136.             if (visit.Position == null)
137.             {
138.                 ScenarioOutput_Latitude.Text   = "No data";
139.                 ScenarioOutput_Longitude.Text  = "No data";
140.                 ScenarioOutput_Accuracy.Text   = "No data";
141.                 ScenarioOutput_IsRemoteSource.Text = "No data";
142.             }
143.             else
144.             {
145.                 ScenarioOutput_Latitude.Text    = visit.Position.Coordinate.Point.Position.Latitude.ToString();
146.                 ScenarioOutput_Longitude.Text   = visit.Position.Coordinate.Point.Position.Longitude.ToString();
147.                 ScenarioOutput_Accuracy.Text    = visit.Position.Coordinate.Accuracy.ToString();
148.                 ScenarioOutput_IsRemoteSource.Text = visit.Position.Coordinate.IsRemoteSource.ToString();
149.             }
150.         }
151.     }

62.             // this does nothing and the user must manually update their preference via Settings.
63.             BackgroundAccessStatus backgroundAccessStatus = await BackgroundExecutionManager.RequestAccessAsync();
64.

66.             // execution, the background task will be ready to run.

80.             // Choosing Venue here as an example.
81.             trigger.MonitoringScope = VisitMonitoringScope.Venue;
82. 
83.             // Associate the trigger with the background task builder
84.             visitTaskBuilder.SetTrigger(trigger);
85. 
86.             // If it is important that there is user presence and/or
87.             // internet connection when OnCompleted is called
88.             // the following could be called before calling Register()
89.             // SystemCondition condition = new SystemCondition(SystemConditionType.UserPresent | SystemConditionType.InternetAvailable);
90.             // visitTaskBuilder.AddCondition(condition);
91.

94. 
95.             // Associate an event handler with the new background task
96.             _visitTask.Completed += OnCompleted;
97. 
98.             UpdateButtonStates();
99.             MainPage.CheckBackgroundAndRequestLocationAccess();
100.         }

1. //*********************************************************
2. //
3. // Copyright (c) Microsoft. All rights reserved.
4. // THIS CODE IS PROVIDED *AS IS* WITHOUT WARRANTY OF
5. // ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING ANY
6. // IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR

10. 
11. using System;
12. using System.Collections.Generic;
13. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Background;
14. using Windows.Data.Json;
15. using Windows.Storage;
16. using Windows.Devices.Geolocation;
17. using Windows.Devices.Geolocation.Geofencing;
18. 
19. namespace BackgroundTask
20. {
21.     // TODO[migrate-014]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
22.     public sealed class GeofenceBackgroundTask : IBackgroundTask
23.     {
24.         // Value determined by how many max length event descriptors (91 chars)
25.         // stored as a JSON string can fit in 8K (max allowed for local settings)
26.         private const int maxEventDescriptors = 42;
27. 
28.         // LocalSettings persistent data.
29.         IDictionary<string, object> _values = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings.Values;
30. 
31.         // TODO[migrate-015]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
32.         void IBackgroundTask.Run(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
33.         {
34.             BackgroundTaskDeferral deferral = taskInstance.GetDeferral();
35. 
36.             try
37.             {
38.                 // Handle geofence state change reports
39.                 ProcessGeofenceStateChangedReports();
40.             }
41.             catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
42.             {
43.                 _values["Status"] = "Location permissions are disabled. Enable access through the settings.";
44.                 _values.Remove("BackgroundGeofenceEventCollection");
45.             }
46.             finally
47.             {
48.                 deferral.Complete();
49.             }
50.         }
51. 
52.         private void ProcessGeofenceStateChangedReports()
53.         {
54.             // Load the values saved from last time.
55.             JsonArray geofenceBackgroundEvents = Helpers.LoadSavedJson("BackgroundGeofenceEventCollection");
56. 
57.             // Process the state changes that have occurred since the last time we ran.
58.             string description = null;
59.             GeofenceMonitor monitor = GeofenceMonitor.Current;
60.             IReadOnlyList<GeofenceStateChangeReport> reports = monitor.ReadReports();
61. 
62.             foreach (GeofenceStateChangeReport report in reports)
63.             {
64.                 description = GenerateReportDescription(report);
65. 
66.                 // Add to the collection, trimming old events if necessary.
67.                 if (geofenceBackgroundEvents.Count == maxEventDescriptors)
68.                 {
69.                     geofenceBackgroundEvents.RemoveAt(maxEventDescriptors - 1);
70.                 }
71.                 geofenceBackgroundEvents.Insert(0, JsonValue.CreateStringValue(description));
72.             }
73. 
74.             int numReports = reports.Count;
75.             if (numReports > 0)
76.             {
77.                 // Save the modified results for next time.
78.                 _values["BackgroundGeofenceEventCollection"] = geofenceBackgroundEvents.Stringify();
79. 
80.                 if (numReports > 1)
81.                 {
82.                     description = $"There are {numReports} new geofence events";
83.                 }
84.                 Helpers.DisplayToast(description);
85.             }
86.         }
87. 
88.         private string GenerateReportDescription(GeofenceStateChangeReport report)
89.         {
90.             GeofenceState state = report.NewState;
91.             DateTimeOffset timestamp = report.Geoposition.Coordinate.Timestamp;
92. 
93.             string result = $"{report.Geofence.Id} {timestamp:G}";
94. 
95.             if (state == GeofenceState.Removed)
96.             {
97.                 GeofenceRemovalReason reason = report.RemovalReason;
98.                 if (reason == GeofenceRemovalReason.Expired)
99.                 {
100.                     result += " (Removed/Expired)";
101.                 }
102.                 else if (reason == GeofenceRemovalReason.Used)
103.                 {
104.                     result += " (Removed/Used)";
105.                 }
106.             }
107.             else if (state == GeofenceState.Entered)
108.             {
109.                 result += " (Entered)";
110.             }
111.             else if (state == GeofenceState.Exited)
112.             {
113.                 result += " (Exited)";
114.             }
115.             return result;
116.         }
117.     }
118. }
119.

11. using System;
12. using Windows.Data.Json;
13. using Windows.Data.Xml.Dom;
14. using Windows.UI.Notifications;
15. 
16. namespace BackgroundTask
17. {
18.     static class Helpers
19.     {
20.         /// <summary>
21.         /// Look up a string in the saved LocalSettings, or a default value if no string is found.
22.         /// </summary>
23.         /// <param name="key">The key to look up</param>
24.         /// <param name="defaultValue">The fallback value to use if the key is not found or the value is not a string</param>
25.         /// <returns></returns>
26.         public static string LookupSavedString(string key, string defaultValue = "")
27.         {
28.             var values = Windows.Storage.ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings.Values;
29.             object o;
30.             values.TryGetValue(key, out o);
31.             string s = o as string;
32.             return (s == null) ? defaultValue : s;
33.         }
34. 
35.         /// <summary>
36.         /// Looks up a JsonArray from the saved LocalSettings, or an empty JsonArray if not found.
37.         /// </summary>
38.         /// <param name="key"></param>
39.         /// <returns></returns>
40.         public static JsonArray LoadSavedJson(string key)
41.         {
42.             string json = LookupSavedString(key);
43.             if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(json))
44.             {
45.                 // Not present. Return an empty array.
46.                 return new JsonArray();
47.             }
48.             else
49.             {
50.                 return JsonValue.Parse(json).GetArray();
51.             }
52.         }
53. 
54. 
55.         /// <summary>
56.         /// Helper method to display a toast notification.
57.         /// </summary>
58.         public static void DisplayToast(string message)
59.         {
60.             // TODO[migrate-016]: see PATTERNS.md#notifications
61.             ToastNotifier ToastNotifier = ToastNotificationManager.CreateToastNotifier();
62.

18. namespace BackgroundTask
19. {
20.     // TODO[migrate-018]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
21.     public sealed class LocationBackgroundTask : IBackgroundTask
22.     {
23.         private CancellationTokenSource _cts = null;
24. 
25.         // LocalSettings persistent data.
26.         IDictionary<string, object> _values = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings.Values;
27. 
28.         // TODO[migrate-019]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
29.         async void IBackgroundTask.Run(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
30.         {
31.             BackgroundTaskDeferral deferral = taskInstance.GetDeferral();
32. 
33.             try
34.             {
35.                 // Associate a cancellation handler with the background task.
36.                 taskInstance.Canceled += OnCanceled;
37. 
38.                 // Get cancellation token
39.                 _cts = new CancellationTokenSource();
40.                 CancellationToken token = _cts.Token;
41. 
42.                 // Create geolocator object
43.                 Geolocator geolocator = new Geolocator();
44. 
45.                 // Make the request for the current position
46.                 Geoposition pos = await geolocator.GetGeopositionAsync().AsTask(token);
47. 
48.                 WriteStatusToAppData($"Time: {DateTime.Now:G}");
49.                 WriteGeolocToAppData(pos);
50.             }
51.             catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
52.             {
53.                 WriteStatusToAppData("Disabled");
54.                 WipeGeolocDataFromAppData();
55.             }
56.             catch (Exception ex)
57.             {
58.                 WriteStatusToAppData(ex.ToString());
59.                 WipeGeolocDataFromAppData();
60.             }
61.             finally
62.             {
63.                 _cts = null;
64.                 deferral.Complete();
65.             }
66.         }
67. 
68.         private void WriteGeolocToAppData(Geoposition pos)
69.         {
70.             _values["Latitude"] = pos.Coordinate.Point.Position.Latitude.ToString();
71.             _values["Longitude"] = pos.Coordinate.Point.Position.Longitude.ToString();
72.             _values["Accuracy"] = pos.Coordinate.Accuracy.ToString();
73.         }
74. 
75.         private void WipeGeolocDataFromAppData()
76.         {
77.             _values.Remove("Latitude");
78.             _values.Remove("Longitude");
79.             _values.Remove("Accuracy");
80.         }
81. 
82.         private void WriteStatusToAppData(string status)
83.         {
84.             _values["Status"] = status;
85.         }
86. 
87.         private void OnCanceled(IBackgroundTaskInstance sender, BackgroundTaskCancellationReason reason)
88.         {
89.             if (_cts != null)
90.             {
91.                 _cts.Cancel();
92.                 _cts = null;
93.             }
94.         }
95.     }
96. }

22.     // TODO[migrate-020]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
23.     public sealed class VisitBackgroundTask : IBackgroundTask
24.     {
25.         // Value determined by how many max length event descriptors (91 chars)
26.         // stored as a JSON string can fit in 8K (max allowed for local settings)
27.         private const int maxEventDescriptors = 42;
28. 
29.         // LocalSettings persistent data.
30.         IDictionary<string, object> _values = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings.Values;
31. 
32.         // TODO[migrate-021]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
33.         void IBackgroundTask.Run(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
34.         {
35.             BackgroundTaskDeferral deferral = taskInstance.GetDeferral();
36.             GeovisitTriggerDetails triggerDetails = (GeovisitTriggerDetails)taskInstance.TriggerDetails;
37. 
38.             // Handle Visit reports
39.             ProcessVisitReports(triggerDetails);
40.             deferral.Complete();
41.         }
42. 
43.         private void ProcessVisitReports(GeovisitTriggerDetails triggerDetails)
44.         {
45.             // Load the values saved from last time.
46.             JsonArray visitBackgroundEvents = Helpers.LoadSavedJson("BackgroundVisitEventCollection");
47. 
48.             // Read reports from the triggerDetails.
49.             IReadOnlyList<Geovisit> reports = triggerDetails.ReadReports();
50. 
51.             string description = null;
52. 
53.             foreach (Geovisit report in reports)
54.             {
55.                 description = GenerateVisitDescription(report);
56. 
57.                 // Add to the collection, trimming old events if necessary.
58.                 if (visitBackgroundEvents.Count == maxEventDescriptors)
59.                 {
60.                     visitBackgroundEvents.RemoveAt(maxEventDescriptors - 1);
61.                 }
62.                 visitBackgroundEvents.Insert(0, JsonValue.CreateStringValue(description));
63.             }
64. 
65.             int numReports = reports.Count;
66.             if (numReports > 0)
67.             {
68.                 // Save the modified results for next time.
69.                 _values["BackgroundVisitEventCollection"] = visitBackgroundEvents.Stringify();
70. 
71.                 if (numReports > 1)
72.                 {
73.                     description = $"There are {numReports} new geofence events";
74.                 }
75.                 Helpers.DisplayToast(description);
76.             }
77.         }
78. 
79.         private string GenerateVisitDescription(Geovisit visit)
80.         {
81.             string result = $"{visit.Timestamp:G} {visit.StateChange}";
82. 
83.             // Check if the report has a valid position.
84.             if (visit.Position != null)
85.             {
86.                 result += $" ({visit.Position.Coordinate.Point.Position.Latitude}, {visit.Position.Coordinate.Point.Position.Longitude})";
87.             }
88. 
89.             return result;
90.         }

<a id="threading"></a>
## Threading: CoreDispatcher → DispatcherQueueUWP (replace this):```csharp
await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => StatusText.Text = "Done");
```WinUI 3:```csharp
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => StatusText.Text = "Done");
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(DispatcherQueuePriority.High, () => ProgressBar.Value = 100);
```Cache the queue off the UI thread via `DispatcherQueue.GetForCurrentThread()`. UWP's ASTA reentrancy protection is gone — watch for reentrancy in async code that pumps messages. See the official [threading guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/threading).
<exited with exit code 0>
--- RESULT ---
<a id="windowing"></a>
## Windowing: Window.Current / ApplicationView / CoreWindow → AppWindow`Window.Current`, `ApplicationView`, and `CoreWindow` are gone. Track the main window yourself and use `Microsoft.UI.Windowing.AppWindow`. See the official [windowing guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/windowing).UWP (replace this):```csharp
var win = Window.Current;
ApplicationView.GetForCurrentView().TryResizeView(new Size(800, 600));
```WinUI 3 — expose `MainWindow` from `App`:```csharp
public partial class App : Application
{
    // With <Nullable>enable</Nullable>, declare as nullable since the assignment only happens during OnLaunched.
    public static Window? MainWindow { get; private set; }    protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs args)
    {
        var window = new MainWindow();
        MainWindow = window;
        window.Activate();
    }
}
```Consumers running after `OnLaunched` can safely null-forgive:```csharp
var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow!);
```Resize via `AppWindow`:```csharp
var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow);
var windowId = Microsoft.UI.Win32Interop.GetWindowIdFromWindow(hwnd);
var appWindow = AppWindow.GetFromWindowId(windowId);
appWindow.Resize(new SizeInt32(800, 600));
```### Window *size* reads: `Window.Current.Bounds` / `CoreWindow.Bounds`UWP also uses `Window.Current.Bounds` / `CoreWindow.Bounds` as a **size source** — most
often for adaptive logic (e.g. the Windows-universal-samples `MainPage.OnNavigatedTo`
auto-selects scenario 0 only when the window is wide enough):```csharp
// UWP
if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640) ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;
else                                   ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
```**Do NOT replace a window-size read with `Page.ActualWidth` / `ActualHeight`.** A page's
`ActualWidth`/`ActualHeight` is **`0` until the first layout pass**, so any size check that
runs in a **constructor** or **`OnNavigatedTo`** sees `0` and silently takes the wrong
branch. In this sample that left `SelectedIndex = -1`, so `ScenarioFrame.Navigate` was
never called and the app launched to an **empty/blank content frame** — a clean build and
a "process stayed alive" smoke launch both pass, only a render check reveals it.Use `AppWindow.Size` (valid immediately, in physical pixels), or defer the size-dependent
logic to a `Loaded` / `SizeChanged` handler where `ActualWidth` is populated:

```csharp
// WinUI 3 — read the live window size up front
var appWindow = App.MainWindow!.AppWindow;          // or GetFromWindowId(...) as above
if (appWindow.Size.Width < 640) ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;
else                            ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
```

Rule of thumb: **window geometry → `AppWindow.Size`; element geometry after layout →
`ActualWidth` from `Loaded`/`SizeChanged`. Never read `ActualWidth` in a constructor or
`OnNavigatedTo`.**

### Initialization order — keep MainWindow's constructor inert

`App.MainWindow` (or any equivalent static window reference) is `null` for the entire duration of `new MainWindow()` — t
the right-hand side runs to completion BEFORE the assignment happens. Anything inside that constructor that reads the sta
atic — directly or transitively — sees `null` and throws `E_POINTER` (0x80004003) at startup. The build is clean; the ana
alyzer is silent; only a runtime launch reveals it.

APIs that commonly trip this race when called from a `Page` reached during `MainWindow` construction:

- `WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow)`
- `XamlRoot` / `Content.XamlRoot` access through a static-window helper
- `PrintManagerInterop.GetForWindow(hwnd)` / `ShowPrintUIForWindowAsync(hwnd)`
- `FileOpenPicker` / `FileSavePicker` / `FolderPicker` (require an HWND)
- `ContentDialog.ShowAsync()` when the dialog's `XamlRoot` is sourced from a static
- `AppWindow.GetFromWindowId(...)` chained through `App.MainWindow`
- Any custom title bar / `SetTitleBar` helper that resolves the HWND statically

Safe pattern — assign before activate, defer first navigation:

```csharp
// App.xaml.cs
protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs args)
{
    var window = new MainWindow();
    MainWindow = window;
    window.Activate();
}
```

The `MainWindow` constructor must do only `InitializeComponent` and inert setup — no navigation to a `Page` that reads `
`App.MainWindow`. Defer the first navigation until after `Activate` completes, either from a `Loaded` handler hooked in `
`InitializeComponent` or by exposing a `NavigateToInitialPage()` method and calling it from `App.OnLaunched` after the st
tatic is assigned.

```csharp
// MainWindow.xaml.cs
public MainWindow()
{
    InitializeComponent();
    this.Activated += (_, _) =>
    {
        if (!_navigated)
        {
            _navigated = true;
            RootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage));
        }
    };
}
private bool _navigated;
```

The validator's 10s smoke launch catches this race **only when the null-static read
reaches the root frame and crashes the process**. When the offending page is reached
through a **nested content `Frame`** (e.g. a `NavigationView` scenario frame), `Frame.Navigate`
marshals the exception into `NavigationFailed` **without terminating the process** — the
smoke launch (and `Validate-UwpMigration.ps1` Section 7) then reports **green on a blank
page**. Do not treat a green smoke launch as proof this race is absent; confirm every
scenario actually renders (see [Silent navigation failures](#silent-navigation-failures)).

### AppWindow API replacements

| UWP API | WinUI 3 API |
|---------|-------------|
| `ApplicationView.TryResizeView` | `AppWindow.Resize` |
| `AppWindow.TryCreateAsync` | `AppWindow.Create` |
| `AppWindow.TryShowAsync` | `AppWindow.Show` |
| `AppWindow.TryConsolidateAsync` | `AppWindow.Destroy` |
| `AppWindow.RequestMoveXxx` | `AppWindow.Move` |
| `AppWindow.RequestPresentation` | `AppWindow.SetPresenter` |
| `CoreApplicationViewTitleBar` | `AppWindowTitleBar` |
| `CoreApplicationView.TitleBar.ExtendViewIntoTitleBar` | `AppWindow.TitleBar.ExtendsContentIntoTitleBar` |

<a id="background-tasks"></a>
## Background Tasks`IBackgroundTask` / `BackgroundTaskRegistration` are not the recommended model. Use the WinAppSDK [`BackgroundTaskBuilder`](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/windows-app-sdk/api/winrt/microsoft.windows.applicationmodel.background.backgroundtaskbuilder) (introduced in 1.7), or move the work to push-driven activation / Windows Task Scheduler. See the [background task migration strategy](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/background-task-migration-strategy).

37.         /// <summary>
38.         /// Look up a background task by name.
39.         /// If found, then also report whether the user has granted background execution access.
40.         /// </summary>
41.         public static IBackgroundTaskRegistration LookupBackgroundTask(string name)
42.         {
43.             IBackgroundTaskRegistration found = null;
44. 
45.             // Loop through all background tasks to find one with the matching name
46.             foreach (var entry in BackgroundTaskRegistration.AllTasks)
47.             {
48.                 var task = entry.Value;
49.                 if (task.Name == name)
50.                 {
51.                     found = task;
52.                     break;
53.                 }
54.             }
55. 
56.             if (found != null)
57.             {
58.                 // Check whether the user has already granted background execution access.
59.                 BackgroundAccessStatus backgroundAccessStatus = BackgroundExecutionManager.GetAccessStatus();
60. 
61.                 MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
62.                 switch (backgroundAccessStatus)
63.                 {
64.                     case BackgroundAccessStatus.AlwaysAllowed:
65.                     case BackgroundAccessStatus.AllowedSubjectToSystemPolicy:
66.                         rootPage.NotifyUser("Background task is already registered. Waiting for next update...", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
67.                         break;
68. 
69.                     default:
70.                         rootPage.NotifyUser("Background tasks may be disabled for this app", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
71.                         break;
72.                 }
73.             }
74. 
75.             return found;
76. 
77.         }
78. 
79.         /// <summary>
80.         /// Check and report whether the user has granted background execution access.
81.         /// If so, then also request location access so that the background task can do its work.
82.         /// </summary>
83.         public static async void CheckBackgroundAndRequestLocationAccess()
84.         {
85.             // Get permission for a background task from the user. If the user has already answered once,
86.             // this does nothing and the user must manually update their preference via Settings.
87.             BackgroundAccessStatus backgroundAccessStatus = await BackgroundExecutionManager.RequestAccessAsync();
88. 
89.             MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
90. 
91.             switch (backgroundAccessStatus)
92.             {
93.                 case BackgroundAccessStatus.AlwaysAllowed:
94.                 case BackgroundAccessStatus.AllowedSubjectToSystemPolicy:
95.                     // BackgroundTask is allowed
96.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("Background task registered.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
97. 
98.                     // Need to request access to location.
99.                     // This must be done when we create the background task registration
100.                     // because the background task cannot display UI.
101.                     // If the user has already answered once,
102.                     // this does nothing and the user must manually update their preference via Settings.
103.                     switch (await Geolocator.RequestAccessAsync())
104.                     {
105.                         case GeolocationAccessStatus.Allowed:
106.                             break;
107. 
108.                         case GeolocationAccessStatus.Denied:
109.                             rootPage.NotifyUser("Access to location is denied.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
110.                             break;
111. 
112.                         case GeolocationAccessStatus.Unspecified:
113.                             rootPage.NotifyUser("Unspecified error!", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
114.                             break;
115.                     }
116.                     break;
117. 
118.                 default:
119.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("Background tasks may be disabled for this app", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
120.                     break;

124.         /// <summary>

126.         /// </summary>

128.         /// <param name="defaultValue">The fallback value to use if the key is not found or the value is not a string</param>
129.         /// <returns></returns>
130.         public static string LookupSavedString(string key, string defaultValue = "")
131.         {
132.             var values = Windows.Storage.ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings.Values;
133.             object o;

135.             string s = o as string;
136.             return (s == null) ? defaultValue : s;

155.                     items.Add(element.GetString());
156.                 }
157.             }
158.         }

22. // The Blank Page item template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=402352&clcid=0x409
23. 
24. namespace SDKTemplate
25. {
26.     /// <summary>
27.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
28.     /// </summary>
29.     public sealed partial class MainPage : Page
30.     {
31.         public static MainPage Current;
32. 
33.         public MainPage()
34.         {
35.             this.InitializeComponent();
36. 
37.             // This is a static public property that allows downstream pages to get a handle to the MainPage instance
38.             // in order to call methods that are in this class.
39.             Current = this;
40.             SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;
41.         }
42. 
43.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
44.         {
45.             // Populate the scenario list from the SampleConfiguration.cs file
46.             var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();
47.             int i = 1;
48.             foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)
49.             {
50.                 itemCollection.Add(new Scenario { Title = $"{i++}) {s.Title}", ClassType = s.ClassType });
51.             }
52.             ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;
53. 
54.             if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
55.             {
56.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;
57.             }
58.             else
59.             {
60.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
61.             }
62.         }
63. 
64.         /// <summary>
65.         /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective
66.         /// sample scenario page.
67.         /// </summary>
68.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
69.         /// <param name="e"></param>
70.         private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
71.         {
72.             // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.
73.             NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
74. 
75.             ListBox scenarioListBox = sender as ListBox;
76.             Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;
77.             if (s != null)
78.             {
79.                 ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);
80.                 if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
81.                 {
82.                     Splitter.IsPaneOpen = false;
83.                 }
84.             }
85.         }
86. 
87.         public List<Scenario> Scenarios
88.         {
89.             get { return this.scenarios; }
90.         }
91. 
92.         /// <summary>
93.         /// Display a message to the user.
94.         /// This method may be called from any thread.
95.         /// </summary>
96.         /// <param name="strMessage"></param>
97.         /// <param name="type"></param>
98.         public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
99.         {
100.             // If called from the UI thread, then update immediately.
101.             // Otherwise, schedule a task on the UI thread to perform the update.
102.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
103.             {
104.                 UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);
105.             }
106.             else
107.             {
108.                 var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));
109.             }
110.         }
111. 
112.         private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
113.         {
114.             switch (type)
115.             {
116.                 case NotifyType.StatusMessage:
117.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Green);
118.                     break;
119.                 case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:
120.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Red);
121.                     break;
122.             }
123. 
124.             StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;
125. 
126.             // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.
127.             StatusBorder.Visibility = (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
128.             if (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty)
129.             {
130.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
131.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
132.             }
133.             else
134.             {
135.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
136.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
137.             }
138. 
139. 			// Raise an event if necessary to enable a screen reader to announce the status update.
140. 			var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);
141. 			if (peer != null)
142. 			{
143. 				peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);
144. 			}
145. 		}
146. 
147.         async void Footer_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
148.         {
149.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(new Uri(((HyperlinkButton)sender).Tag.ToString()));
150.         }
151. 
152.         private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
153.         {
154.             Splitter.IsPaneOpen = !Splitter.IsPaneOpen;
155.         }
156.     }
157.     public enum NotifyType
158.     {
159.         StatusMessage,
160.         ErrorMessage
161.     };
162. }
